# Protocol Documentation

This reference is generated from the protobuf files under `protos/` using
[protoc-gen-doc](https://github.com/pseudomuto/protoc-gen-doc). **Do not edit this file manually.**
After editing any `.proto` definitions, run the following commands:

```bash
BUF_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=config/protobuf/buf.work.yaml buf lint
./gradlew generateProto
./dev-tools/docs/generate-grpc-docs.sh
```

to regenerate these docs and verify schema/doc consistency.

Refer to the [gRPC API Style Guide](../architecture/system-architecture-grpc.md)
for conventions on schema evolution and error handling. See each service's
`README.md` under `services/` for high-level explanations of its endpoints.

## account/v1/account_gameplay_admission_lease.proto



<a name="account-v1-AbortGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest"></a>

### AbortGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest
A missing decision UUID remains UNKNOWN, never NO_TOKEN or completed cleanup.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| presented_initial_gsa_compact_jwt | [string](#string) | repeated | Transient credential input, bounded by the existing GSA profile; never echoed in a response. |
| original_lease_evidence_utf8 | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |
| binding_decision_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |






<a name="account-v1-AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest"></a>

### AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest
Distinct negative purpose: never accepts or manufactures a positive admission decision.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| presented_initial_gsa_compact_jwt | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| original_lease_evidence_utf8 | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |
| transition_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| abandonment_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |






<a name="account-v1-AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse"></a>

### AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse
ABORTED-only retained original terminal and pending cleanup linkage, never cleanup completion.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| original_lease_evidence_utf8 | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |
| state | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseState](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseState) | repeated |  |
| transition_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| abandonment_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| candidate_sha256 | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| closure_readback_sha256 | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| orphan_cleanup_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| negative_resolution_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |






<a name="account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionAbortResolutionEvidence"></a>

### AccountGameplayAdmissionAbortResolutionEvidence
Separate durable Account resolution for an unchanged original ABORTED/null-decision lease.
Repeated fields preserve absence and duplicates for closed receiver validation.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| resolution_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| original_lease_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| original_lease_fence | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| original_lease_sha256 | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| orphan_cleanup_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| binding_decision_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |






<a name="account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse"></a>

### AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse
Lease operations return the same closed original-operation readback. Repeated fields preserve
absence and duplicates for receiver validation. The lease bytes remain unchanged and complete.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| original_lease_evidence_utf8 | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |
| state | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseState](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseState) | repeated |  |
| binding_decision_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| orphan_cleanup_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated | ABORTED-only pending cleanup obligation; this never reports cleanup completion. |
| original_ack_receipt | [AccountGameplayAdmissionOriginalAckReceiptEvidence](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionOriginalAckReceiptEvidence) | repeated |  |
| original_new_credential | [OriginalNewGameplayCredentialResult](#account-v1-OriginalNewGameplayCredentialResult) | repeated | Present only on IssueOriginalNewGameplayCredential. Kept separate so closed original lease evidence and its committed receipt remain byte-for-byte unchanged. |
| abort_resolution | [AccountGameplayAdmissionAbortResolutionEvidence](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionAbortResolutionEvidence) | repeated | Abort-only reconciliation of an originally unknown decision. This preserves the original terminal fields and reports a pending obligation, never completed cleanup or admission. |






<a name="account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionOriginalAckReceiptEvidence"></a>

### AccountGameplayAdmissionOriginalAckReceiptEvidence
Complete durable AccountGameplayAdmissionOriginalAckReceipt shape. The operation identity and
state are supplied by the enclosing response. These values are source evidence, not a serialized
in-process OriginalCommitAcknowledgement or standalone admission authority.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| request_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| account_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| lease_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| lease_fence | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| evidence_sha256 | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| binding_decision_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| expires_at_ms | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| finalization_xid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| committed_before_ms | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| receipt_xid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |






<a name="account-v1-AcquireGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest"></a>

### AcquireGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest
A new operation selector and expected current values only; Account still resolves and checks
every owner. These fields do not authorize or allocate a lease.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| request_uuid | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| presented_initial_gsa_compact_jwt | [string](#string) | repeated | Transient credential input, bounded by the existing GSA profile; never echoed in a response. |
| expected_candidate | [game_session.v1.CanonicalGameplayAdmissionCandidate](#game_session-v1-CanonicalGameplayAdmissionCandidate) | repeated |  |
| expected_target | [game_session.v1.CanonicalPlayableTargetEvidence](#game_session-v1-CanonicalPlayableTargetEvidence) | repeated |  |
| binding_generation | [string](#string) | repeated |  |

<a name="account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseService"></a>

### AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseService
Explicit, manually composed Account-only NEW-binding owner protocol. The same-namespace
Game Session certificate is the workload identity; no generic service JWT is accepted as it.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLease | [AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest](#account-v1-AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest) | [AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse](#account-v1-AbortNeverAdmittedGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse) |  |
| AcquireGameplayAdmissionLease | [AcquireGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest](#account-v1-AcquireGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest) | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse) |  |
| FinalizeGameplayAdmissionLease | [FinalizeGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest](#account-v1-FinalizeGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest) | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse) |  |
| ReadGameplayAdmissionLease | [ReadGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest](#account-v1-ReadGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest) | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse) |  |
| AbortGameplayAdmissionLease | [AbortGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest](#account-v1-AbortGameplayAdmissionLeaseRequest) | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse) |  |
| IssueOriginalNewGameplayCredential | [IssueOriginalNewGameplayCredentialRequest](#account-v1-IssueOriginalNewGameplayCredentialRequest) | [AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse](#account-v1-AccountGameplayAdmissionLeaseResponse) | Issues the first purpose-bound credential only after Account&#39;s original COMMITTED lease and independent original-ACK receipt have been re-read. The returned handle is internal to Game Session and is never forwarded to a player client. |
| ConfirmOriginalNewGameplayAdmission | [ConfirmOriginalNewGameplayAdmissionRequest](#account-v1-ConfirmOriginalNewGameplayAdmissionRequest) | [OriginalNewAdmissionConfirmationResponse](#account-v1-OriginalNewAdmissionConfirmationResponse) | Requests exact recovery of the final Account confirmation. Account performs the protected original-final-admission read itself; this request carries no proof or timestamps. |

<a name="account-v1-GameplayLoginTransportClass"></a>

### GameplayLoginTransportClass
Credential-bearing LOGIN accepts only the authenticated TCP Proxy branch.
FIRST_PARTY_WEB remains bootstrap-backed and cannot present credentials here.

| Name | Number | Description |
| ---- | ------ | ----------- |
| GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_TRANSPORT_CLASS_UNSPECIFIED | 0 |  |
| GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_TRANSPORT_CLASS_TRUSTED_TCP_PROXY | 1 |  |
| GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_TRANSPORT_CLASS_FIRST_PARTY_WEB | 2 |  |

### InitialGameplayLoginDisposalDisposition


| Name | Number | Description |
| ---- | ------ | ----------- |
| INITIAL_GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_DISPOSAL_DISPOSITION_UNSPECIFIED | 0 |  |
| INITIAL_GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_DISPOSAL_DISPOSITION_PENDING | 1 |  |
| INITIAL_GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_DISPOSAL_DISPOSITION_RETIRED | 2 |  |
| INITIAL_GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_DISPOSAL_DISPOSITION_NO_TOKEN | 3 | No registry-bound/exposed usable issuance exists or can later be created for this attempt. This does not claim that transient token bytes were never signed. |



<a name="account-v1-InitialGameplayLoginDisposalProofKind"></a>

| INITIAL_GAMEPLAY_LOGIN_DISPOSAL_PROOF_KIND_NO_ISSUANCE_BINDING | 2 |  |

realm is public, that its entry policy is PRESEEDED_ONLY, or that gameplay admission is allowed.

| GENERATION_RECORDED | 3 |  |







<a name="account-v1-AccountJwtSignerMaterializationService"></a>

### AccountJwtSignerMaterializationService
This exact authenticated peer performs only fixed-name Secret observation, pending-slot key
generation, and an operation-bound private-slot transition after Account durably authorizes a
PREPARED promotion. Account alone prepares/commits/promotes the lifecycle and publishes JWKS.

| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| EnrollBootstrap | [EnrollBootstrapRequest](#account-v1-EnrollBootstrapRequest) | [EnrollBootstrapResponse](#account-v1-EnrollBootstrapResponse) |  |
| GetCurrentGenerationRequest | [GetCurrentGenerationRequestRequest](#account-v1-GetCurrentGenerationRequestRequest) | [GetCurrentGenerationRequestResponse](#account-v1-GetCurrentGenerationRequestResponse) |  |
| RecordSecretObservation | [RecordSecretObservationRequest](#account-v1-RecordSecretObservationRequest) | [GetCurrentGenerationRequestResponse](#account-v1-GetCurrentGenerationRequestResponse) |  |
| RecordGenerationResult | [RecordGenerationResultRequest](#account-v1-RecordGenerationResultRequest) | [RecordGenerationResultResponse](#account-v1-RecordGenerationResultResponse) |  |
| GetCurrentPromotionRequest | [GetCurrentPromotionRequestRequest](#account-v1-GetCurrentPromotionRequestRequest) | [GetCurrentPromotionRequestResponse](#account-v1-GetCurrentPromotionRequestResponse) |  |
| RecordPrivatePromotionResult | [RecordPrivatePromotionResultRequest](#account-v1-RecordPrivatePromotionResultRequest) | [RecordPrivatePromotionResultResponse](#account-v1-RecordPrivatePromotionResultResponse) |  |

<a name="account_v1_start_session_activation_protection_acquisition-proto"></a>

## account/v1/start_session_activation_protection_acquisition.proto



<a name="account-v1-AcquireOriginalStartSessionActivationProtectionRequest"></a>

### AcquireOriginalStartSessionActivationProtectionRequest
Each repeated field must occur exactly once. Repetition is intentional: protobuf singular
fields discard duplicate occurrences during parsing, while these closed transport fields must
reject duplicates at the receiver.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [int32](#int32) | repeated |  |
| target_namespace | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| original_post_authorization_tuple | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |
| game_session_owner_mutation_id | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| game_session_owner_attempt_id | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| game_session_owner_fence | [string](#string) | repeated | Canonical positive unsigned-decimal text; parsed only if it fits a positive signed Java long. |
| activation_request_id | [string](#string) | repeated |  |
| world_preparing_evidence | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated | Exact complete canonical World PREPARING evidence. The 64 MiB limit belongs only to this new Account activation carrier and does not widen the World lifecycle API&#39;s limits. |






<a name="account-v1-AcquireOriginalStartSessionActivationProtectionResponse"></a>

### AcquireOriginalStartSessionActivationProtectionResponse



| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| evidence_canonical_bytes | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated | Exactly one complete canonical AccountStartSessionActivationProtectionEvidence value. Its existing 200 MiB limit is preserved; this RPC adds only protobuf framing headroom. |












<a name="account-v1-AccountStartSessionActivationProtectionAcquisitionService"></a>

### AccountStartSessionActivationProtectionAcquisitionService
Standalone, unregistered World-to-Account source-protection acquisition for the original
StartSession activation. It does not activate World or settle/release Account protection.

<a name="automation_scripting-v1-TriggerScriptEventRequest"></a>

### TriggerScriptEventRequest



| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| tenant_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| game_instance_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| region_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| region_epoch | [int64](#int64) |  | Required as a positive value for every gameplay/runtime and scheduler trigger so triggers are fenced across scoped coordination resets. Explicit out-of-band non-runtime triggers may omit region_epoch only when their owner contract documents the consistency model; the tenant-readiness onLoad branch is pre-instance and omits runtime region identity. |
| entity_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| script_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| plugin_id | [string](#string) |  | Plugin identity (required for plugin triggers). For core script triggers, these fields must be absent. |
| plugin_version_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| event_type | [string](#string) |  |  |
| script_patch_version | [string](#string) |  |  |
| script_event_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| is_dry_run | [bool](#bool) |  |  |
| trigger_mode | [TriggerMode](#automation_scripting-v1-TriggerMode) |  |  |
| due_tick_id | [int64](#int64) |  | Scheduling context (when applicable). |
| due_at_ms | [int64](#int64) |  |  |
| payload_json | [string](#string) |  | Event payload envelope (implementation-specific). |
| event_schema_version | [string](#string) |  | Registry-controlled schema selector for custom and service-specific events. Built-in events may default to &#34;v1&#34; when omitted. |
| read_snapshot_token | [string](#string) |  | Required when the event registry marks the event as producer-supplied authoritative snapshot. The token format is registry/event-specific. |
| playable_state_scope | [entity_management.v1.PlayableStateScope](#entity_management-v1-PlayableStateScope) |  | Gameplay-originated triggers should carry the resolved playable-state namespace explicitly so downstream scripting work does not infer shared-vs-isolated roster policy from instance ids alone. Non-gameplay/global triggers may leave this unspecified. |
| world_slug | [string](#string) |  | Gameplay-originated triggers should also preserve the admitted routing bundle minted during login/bootstrap/PLAY so later scripting retries, dead letters, and handoffs do not collapse back to runtime-id-only identity. |
| realm_slug | [string](#string) |  |  |
| pointer_version | [string](#string) |  |  |
| script_pin_epoch | [int64](#int64) |  | Exact Game Session source pin generation captured when this trigger was created. Required as a positive value for every instance-scoped gameplay/runtime or scheduler trigger, and paired with a nonblank script_pin_control_plane_request_id; omitted with the entire tuple only for pre-instance ingress such as tenant-readiness onLoad. Epoch zero never admits an instance-scoped gameplay/runtime or scheduler trigger. |
| script_pin_control_plane_request_id | [string](#string) |  | Canonical control-plane request identity paired with script_pin_epoch. Required and nonblank for every instance-scoped gameplay/runtime or scheduler trigger; omitted with the entire tuple only for pre-instance ingress such as tenant-readiness onLoad. |
| script_patch_base_version_id | [int64](#int64) |  | Immutable base context paired with the script patch. Required and positive for every script-patch trigger, including pre-instance readiness work; it is provenance, not part of Trigger Identity or the Game Session pin epoch fence. |






<a name="automation_scripting-v1-TriggerScriptEventResponse"></a>

### TriggerScriptEventResponse

### AutomationScriptingService


| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| Ping | [PingRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-PingRequest) | [PingResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-PingResponse) | Simple ping used by health checks |
| TriggerScriptEvent | [TriggerScriptEventRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-TriggerScriptEventRequest) | [TriggerScriptEventResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-TriggerScriptEventResponse) | Deliver a script trigger for evaluation. This RPC is idempotent with respect to Trigger Identity (including script_event_id) and must not evaluate the DSL body more than once for the same identity. |
| CreateFormation | [CreateFormationRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-CreateFormationRequest) | [CreateFormationResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-CreateFormationResponse) | Creates a new NPC formation |
| AddFormationMember | [AddFormationMemberRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-AddFormationMemberRequest) | [AddFormationMemberResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-AddFormationMemberResponse) | Adds an NPC to an existing formation |
| ListFormationMembers | [ListFormationMembersRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-ListFormationMembersRequest) | [ListFormationMembersResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-ListFormationMembersResponse) | Lists all NPCs belonging to a formation |
| UpdateScript | [UpdateScriptRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-UpdateScriptRequest) | [UpdateScriptResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-UpdateScriptResponse) | Uploads or replaces a script definition |
| GetScriptStatus | [GetScriptStatusRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-GetScriptStatusRequest) | [GetScriptStatusResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-GetScriptStatusResponse) | Checks if a script is queued or currently running |
| NotifyScriptVersionUpdate | [NotifyScriptVersionUpdateRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-NotifyScriptVersionUpdateRequest) | [NotifyScriptVersionUpdateResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-NotifyScriptVersionUpdateResponse) | Signals that a new script patch version is available |
| ObserveRuntimeTickProgress | [ObserveRuntimeTickProgressRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-ObserveRuntimeTickProgressRequest) | [ObserveRuntimeTickProgressResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-ObserveRuntimeTickProgressResponse) | Internal Game Session heartbeat/progress feed for tick-aligned script schedules. |
| GetDraftDesignDigest | [GetDraftDesignDigestRequest](#automation_scripting-v1-GetDraftDesignDigestRequest) | [GetDraftDesignDigestResponse](#automation_scripting-v1-GetDraftDesignDigestResponse) | Returns the canonical design-time digest for script definitions that participate in one script-patch version. |

<a name="entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterSelectedAssignmentResponse"></a>

### CanonicalGameplayRosterSelectedAssignmentResponse



| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| request_uuid | [string](#string) |  |  |
| canonical_account_uuid | [string](#string) |  |  |
| selected_character_uuid | [string](#string) |  |  |

| assignment_uuid | [string](#string) |  | Original non-nil operation identity and immutable intent digest from the unique committed ASSIGNED PRESEEDED PLAYER receipt matching this exact account, actor, target, and owner proof. This read returns persisted owner evidence; it never mints an assignment identity or digest. |
| intent_digest | [string](#string) |  |  |
| snapshot | [CanonicalGameplayRosterSnapshotReference](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterSnapshotReference) |  | Exact snapshot reference supplied by the caller and validated by this operation. The response echoes that binding alongside the original persisted assignment reference. The reference does not prove admission or current Account eligibility; placement must revalidate it with its owning Entity write/readback contract. |

<a name="entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterService"></a>

### CanonicalGameplayRosterService
Canonical gameplay roster discovery is a pre-admission, non-admitting boundary. The two
preseeded methods below remain restricted to PRESEEDED_ONLY; other policy values are
non-authorizing and fail with FAILED_PRECONDITION. The PLAYER_CREATED methods are a separate
source-bound read contract and do not authenticate their unsigned context or authorize the
legacy numeric ListCharactersByAccount RPC for canonical gameplay. A valid ListPreseededRoster
response may contain zero actors; that is a successful lookup. These RPCs use canonical non-OK gRPC statuses
rather than in-response errors: malformed requests are INVALID_ARGUMENT, stale snapshots or
selections not present in the bound snapshot are FAILED_PRECONDITION, unauthorized callers are
UNAUTHENTICATED or PERMISSION_DENIED as applicable, and unavailable dependencies are
UNAVAILABLE. No partial success is returned.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| ListPreseededRoster | [CanonicalGameplayRosterRequest](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterRequest) | [CanonicalGameplayRosterResponse](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterResponse) | Returns the persisted preseeded roster for the exact expected target. The server requires an authenticated, server-derived PlayerExecutionContext and exact same-namespace Game Session mTLS caller allowlisting. Context account_id equals canonical_account_uuid; request_id equals request_uuid; tenant_id, realm_id, playable_state_namespace_id, playable_state_scope, and game_instance_id equal the corresponding expected_target values; session_id identifies the authenticated session. Context playable_state_scope is the exact text &#34;SHARED&#34; or &#34;ISOLATED&#34; corresponding to the target enum value; numeric enum text is invalid. No character_id is selected yet. The target policy must be PRESEEDED_ONLY. These equality checks do not require an additional Account lookup. A successful response includes its immutable snapshot UUID and digest, including when the roster is empty. |
| ReadSelectedPreseededAssignment | [CanonicalGameplayRosterSelectedAssignmentRequest](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterSelectedAssignmentRequest) | [CanonicalGameplayRosterSelectedAssignmentResponse](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalGameplayRosterSelectedAssignmentResponse) | Reads the selected assignment only if the supplied snapshot UUID and digest still identify the roster from which selected_character_uuid was chosen. The response echoes that exact snapshot reference. A changed snapshot or selection absent from it fails with FAILED_PRECONDITION. It has the same authenticated context and exact target equality requirements as ListPreseededRoster, and context.character_id must equal selected_character_uuid. The target policy must be PRESEEDED_ONLY. |
| ListPlayerCreatedRoster | [CanonicalPlayerCreatedRosterRequest](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalPlayerCreatedRosterRequest) | [CanonicalPlayerCreatedRosterResponse](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalPlayerCreatedRosterResponse) | Reads the source-bound persisted PLAYER_CREATED roster. The unsigned execution context is equality evidence only; peer authentication remains a server responsibility. |
| ReadSelectedPlayerCreatedCharacter | [CanonicalSelectedPlayerCreatedCharacterRequest](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalSelectedPlayerCreatedCharacterRequest) | [CanonicalSelectedPlayerCreatedCharacterResponse](#entity_management-v1-CanonicalSelectedPlayerCreatedCharacterResponse) | Reads the exact selected actor from the original source-bound snapshot and returns the persisted creation terminal. This carrier does not authenticate that terminal. |

<a name="entity_management-v1-CanonicalPreseededActorAssignmentService"></a>

### CanonicalPreseededActorAssignmentService
Dedicated run-owned bootstrap ingress. This operation creates only a non-admitting
PRESEEDED_ONLY actor; it does not authorize PLAY or runtime activation.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| AssignPreseededActor | [AssignPreseededActorRequest](#entity_management-v1-AssignPreseededActorRequest) | [AssignPreseededActorResponse](#entity_management-v1-AssignPreseededActorResponse) |  |
| ReadPreseededActorAssignmentReceipt | [ReadPreseededActorAssignmentReceiptRequest](#entity_management-v1-ReadPreseededActorAssignmentReceiptRequest) | [ReadPreseededActorAssignmentReceiptResponse](#entity_management-v1-ReadPreseededActorAssignmentReceiptResponse) |  |
| ReadPreseededActorRuntimeReference | [ReadPreseededActorRuntimeReferenceRequest](#entity_management-v1-ReadPreseededActorRuntimeReferenceRequest) | [ReadPreseededActorRuntimeReferenceResponse](#entity_management-v1-ReadPreseededActorRuntimeReferenceResponse) | Exact same-namespace Game Session purpose; never allocates a missing projection. |

rechecks the original Account operation. This read is evidence, not admission.

<a name="game_design-v1-GameDesignStartSessionLaunchDescriptorService"></a>

### GameDesignStartSessionLaunchDescriptorService
Standalone exact-replay descriptor projection for the existing authorized StartSession flow.
The server authenticates the same-namespace Game Session workload before decoding or invoking
the producer. This service is deliberately separate from the read-only association service.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| ResolveStartSessionLaunchDescriptor | [ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociationRequest](#game_design-v1-ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociationRequest) | [ResolveStartSessionLaunchDescriptorResponse](#game_design-v1-ResolveStartSessionLaunchDescriptorResponse) |  |


<a name="game_design-v1-GameDesignStartSessionTemplateAssociationReadService"></a>

### GameDesignStartSessionTemplateAssociationReadService
Standalone read-only handoff. Authenticate the exact same-namespace Game Session
peer before decoding, then independently verify the original redeemed Account
operation before reading content. This does not authorize source mutation,
descriptor mutation, World preparation, or admission.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociation | [ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociationRequest](#game_design-v1-ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociationRequest) | [ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociationResponse](#game_design-v1-ReadStartSessionTemplateAssociationResponse) |  |

<a name="game_session-v1-GetCanonicalInitialAdmissionOwnerProofResponse"></a>

### GetCanonicalInitialAdmissionOwnerProofResponse
Every successful domain result echoes the complete expected operation tuple. Only a COMMITTED
pointer/audit result or a positively fenced durable ABORTED result is terminal proof. Missing
owner attempts and reads that cannot establish an exact result use non-OK gRPC status, never a
NOT_FOUND/UNAVAILABLE outcome or an inferred abort.

| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| target_namespace | [string](#string) |  |  |
| initial_admission_request_id | [string](#string) |  |  |

| canonical_tenant_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| world_slug | [string](#string) |  |  |
| realm_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| playable_state_namespace_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| playable_state_scope | [string](#string) |  |  |
| canonical_game_instance_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| canonical_version_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| active_lifecycle_epoch | [int64](#int64) |  |  |
| expected_catalog_revision | [int64](#int64) |  |  |
| origin | [CanonicalInitialAdmissionOrigin](#game_session-v1-CanonicalInitialAdmissionOrigin) |  |  |
| expected_prior_pointer_version | [int64](#int64) | optional |  |
| hold_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| hold_fence | [string](#string) |  |  |
| hold_binding_digest | [string](#string) |  |  |
| outcome | [CanonicalInitialAdmissionOwnerProofOutcome](#game_session-v1-CanonicalInitialAdmissionOwnerProofOutcome) |  |  |
| committed_pointer_version | [int64](#int64) | optional |  |
| audit_event_id | [int64](#int64) | optional |  |
| proof_digest | [string](#string) |  | Terminal Game Session proof digest; sha256:-prefixed and distinct from the bare request digest. |
| positive_durable_abort | [bool](#bool) |  |  |
| terminal_at | [google.protobuf.Timestamp](#google-protobuf-Timestamp) |  |  |

<a name="game_session-v1-ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionRequest"></a>

### ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionRequest
The selector carries Account&#39;s expected immutable anchors, never a raw token or rebind handle.
Repeated fields let the receiver reject both absence and duplicate wire values.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| expected_original_admission_json | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |






<a name="game_session-v1-ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionResponse"></a>

### ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionResponse
Game Session echoes the exact selector and returns closed evidence decoded from the retained
candidate plus the actual persistent finalized session readback. The result contains digests,
never token or handle bytes.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| schema_version | [uint32](#uint32) | repeated |  |
| expected_original_admission_json | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |
| actual_final_admission_json | [bytes](#bytes) | repeated |  |












<a name="game_session-v1-OriginalNewFinalAdmissionReadService"></a>

Separate manually composed Account-only read of the actual finalized ORIGINAL_NEW Redis value.

| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmission | [ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionRequest](#game_session-v1-ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionRequest) | [ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionResponse](#game_session-v1-ReadOriginalNewFinalAdmissionResponse) |  |

<a name="tcp-proxy_v1_tcp_proxy_service-proto"></a>
<p align="right"><a href="#top">Top</a></p>

## tcp-proxy/v1/tcp_proxy_service.proto



<a name="tcp_proxy-v1-NotifyDisconnectRequest"></a>

### NotifyDisconnectRequest
Identifies a Telnet connection that has closed, along with enough context for the Game Session Service
to treat the signal as an advisory hint.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| session_id | [string](#string) |  | Optional proxy-owned hidden metadata derived from the canonical Telnet SESSION envelope. It is not client-supplied player-attach input and is advisory only; Game Session must validate tenant/session claims against its own state. session_id corresponds to the gameplay session identifier used by the Telnet path and consumers should treat it as opaque. |
| tenant_id | [string](#string) |  |  |
| proxy_connection_id | [string](#string) |  | Required, server-generated identifier for the Telnet connection. This value is generated by the TCP Proxy on accept and is propagated to the Gateway/Game Session layer as a WebSocket handshake header (for example, X-Proxy-Connection-Id) so Game Session can associate it with an authenticated session. |
| disconnect_sequence | [uint64](#uint64) |  | Required, monotonically increasing sequence number scoped to proxy_connection_id. This allows Game Session to deduplicate at-least-once delivery (including retries and out-of-order arrivals). |
| game_instance_id | [string](#string) |  | Optional, explicit game instance identifier carried by the canonical Telnet SESSION envelope. |






<a name="tcp_proxy-v1-NotifyDisconnectResponse"></a>

### NotifyDisconnectResponse
Error is empty on success. When populated, it contains a logical failure
(for example, unknown session) while the transport status remains OK.


| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| error | [shared.v1.ErrorDetail](#shared-v1-ErrorDetail) |  |  |






<a name="tcp_proxy-v1-PingRequest"></a>

### PingRequest







<a name="tcp_proxy-v1-PingResponse"></a>

### PingResponse



| Field | Type | Label | Description |
| ----- | ---- | ----- | ----------- |
| message | [string](#string) |  |  |












<a name="tcp_proxy-v1-TcpProxyService"></a>

### TcpProxyService
TcpProxyService defines internal-only gRPC endpoints used by the TCP Proxy and Game Session services.

Directionality and ownership:
- Ping is implemented and served by the TCP Proxy Service itself for operator tooling and tests.
- NotifyDisconnect is implemented and served by the Game Session Service as an internal-only event sink
  that the TCP Proxy calls when Telnet connections close.

Even though both RPCs live on the same logical TcpProxyService definition for schema/versioning purposes,
they are hosted by different microservices. Callers must treat Ping and NotifyDisconnect as separate
deployment targets following the ownership rules described in the TCP Proxy and Game Session design docs.

These RPCs are not exposed through Spring Cloud Gateway and are intended for use by backend services and tooling only.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| Ping | [PingRequest](#tcp_proxy-v1-PingRequest) | [PingResponse](#tcp_proxy-v1-PingResponse) | Simple connectivity check used by operators and tests. |
| NotifyDisconnect | [NotifyDisconnectRequest](#tcp_proxy-v1-NotifyDisconnectRequest) | [NotifyDisconnectResponse](#tcp_proxy-v1-NotifyDisconnectResponse) | Emits a best-effort, at-least-once lifecycle event whenever a Telnet connection closes.

Semantics and failure expectations: - Events may be delivered more than once; the Game Session Service must treat them as idempotent. - The primary idempotency key is {proxy_connection_id, disconnect_sequence}. - session_id and tenant_id may be omitted when the proxy did not receive the canonical Telnet SESSION envelope; when present, they are proxy-owned hidden transport metadata, not player attach input. - The proxy does not buffer or replay gameplay commands; it only signals disconnects. Command queues remain owned by Game Session and Redis. - Transport-level failures surface as standard gRPC statuses; logical failures are reported via the ErrorDetail in NotifyDisconnectResponse. - Lost or delayed events must not cause stuck or &#34;ghost&#34; sessions: Game Session is responsible for independently detecting liveness via its own mechanisms (for example, WebSocket/TCP close and Redis timeouts). In the worst case, losing a NotifyDisconnect should only slow down cleanup, not leave players incorrectly logged in or unable to resume. |

<a name="world_management-v1-WorldSelectedDraftPublicationFreezeService"></a>

### WorldSelectedDraftPublicationFreezeService
Standalone Game Design-to-World selected-publication freeze transport. The authenticated
producer sends the exact Account order and immutable selection digest; World resolves the
retained APPLIED graph and derives its private owner binding locally.

| Method Name | Request Type | Response Type | Description |
| ----------- | ------------ | ------------- | ------------|
| BeginVersionPublicationFreeze | [BeginVersionPublicationFreezeRequest](#world_management-v1-BeginVersionPublicationFreezeRequest) | [BeginVersionPublicationFreezeResponse](#world_management-v1-BeginVersionPublicationFreezeResponse) |  |
| ReadSelectedPublicationArtifactInventory | [ReadSelectedPublicationArtifactInventoryRequest](#world_management-v1-ReadSelectedPublicationArtifactInventoryRequest) | [ReadSelectedPublicationArtifactInventoryResponse](#world_management-v1-ReadSelectedPublicationArtifactInventoryResponse) |  |
